BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                   業務回顧
On behalf of the board of directors (the “Board”), I am pleased to report to      本人謹代表董事會（「董事會」），欣然向各位股
our shareholders the results of Kingboard Laminates Holdings Limited and its      東公佈，建滔積層板控股有限公司及其附屬公
subsidiaries (the “Group”) for the financial year ended 31 December 2020.         司（「本集團」）截至二零二零年十二月三十一日
                                                                                  止財政年度之全年業績。

2020 was a challenge-ridden year with the spread of the coronavirus               二零二零年是極具挑戰的一年，新冠病毒疫情
pandemic, putting downward pressure on the global economy. In spite of            的蔓延，令全球經濟面臨下行壓力。儘管宏觀
the unfavourable macro-environment, the electronics industry performed            環境不利，整體電子行業表現仍然卓越。電腦
well. Aided by increased remote work and time spent staying at home, the          及新型家電類產品受惠於遠程辦公，居家時間
demands for computers and new home electronic appliances flourished.              增長等因素，需求蓬勃向上。過往的熱門產品
Popular products in the past like cars and phones also saw their demands          如汽車及手機等，需求亦於下半年回復升軌。
back on the growth track in the second half of the year. The electronics          電子產業正以較全球經濟更先和更快的步伐回
industry is rebounding at a pace ahead of the world economy.                      升。

integrated supply chain, the Group, on the one hand, rapidly enhanced the         步提升產品價格，多措並舉收穫顯著成效。覆

this business segment’s profitability was also on track for strong growth.        集團全年營業額因此下降6%至一百七十三億

sales of remaining units, its turnover was lower compared to that of 2019,        始穩步提升產品價格，加上其出色的成本控

The Group continued to adopt a prudent financial management policy. The            本集團繼續採取審慎的財務政策，並無訂立任
Group did not enter into any material types of derivative financial instrument,    何重要的衍生金融工具，亦無面對重大的外匯
nor did it have any material foreign exchange exposure during the year. The        風險，收入主要以港元、人民幣及美元結算，

dollars, was fairly matched with the currency requirements of its operating

The board of Directors (the “Board”) of the Company recognises the                 本公司董事會（「董事會」）明暸上市公司企業管
importance of corporate governance practice of a listed company. It is in          治常規之重要性。上市公司之營運具透明度，
the interest of the stakeholders and shareholders for a listed company to          採納各種自行規管政策與程序以及監控機制，
operate in a transparent manner with the adoptions of various self-regulatory      並清楚界定董事與管理層權責，乃符合權益持
policies, procedures and monitoring mechanisms and a clear definition of           有人及股東之利益。
accountability of directors and management.

The Company has complied with the code provisions as set out in the Code           除非執行董事及獨立非執行董事並非根據守則
throughout the year ended 31 December 2020, save for the deviation from            第A.4.1條條文按特定任期委任之偏離情況之
paragraph A.4.1 of the Code since the non-executive Director and the               外，本公司於截至二零二零年十二月三十一日
independent non-executive Directors are not appointed for a specific term.         止年度已一直遵守守則所載守則條文。儘管有

executive Director and independent non-executive Directors) are subject            立非執行董事）均遵照本公司組織章程細則輪席
to retirement by rotation and re-election at the Company’s annual general          退任，並可於本公司股東週年大會重選連任。

the Company considers that steps have been taken with a view to ensuring           公司的企業管治常規符合守則原則。

AUDITOR'S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE                                                 核數師就審計綜合財務報表
AUDIT OF THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                                                承擔的責任
STATEMENTS
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the                我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存
consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material                在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s report      合理保證，並按照我們商定的業務約定條款僅
that includes our opinion solely to you, as a body, in accordance with our         向全體股東出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。
agreed terms of engagement, and for no other purpose. We do not assume             除此以外，我們的報告不可用作其他用途。我
responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents    們並不就本報告之內容對任何其他人士承擔任
of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not      何責任或接受任何義務。合理保證是高水準的
a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will always           保證，但不能保證按照《香港審計準則》進行的
detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from        審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。
fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate,   錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預
they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of           期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表
users taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.               使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，
                                                                                   則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。

                                                                                   們亦：

•      Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the               •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜合
       consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error,               財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設
       design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks,                  計及執行審計程序以應對這些風險，以

       provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material           們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串
       misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from         謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或淩
       error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional omissions,          駕於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺
       misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.                        詐而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未

1.    GENERAL                                                                    1.   一般事項
      Kingboard Laminates Holdings Limited (the “Company”) was                        建滔積層板控股有限公司（「本公司」）在
      incorporated and registered as an exempted company with limited                 開曼群島註冊成立並登記為一家獲豁免
      liability in the Cayman Islands and its shares are listed on the main           之有限公司，其股份於香港聯合交易所
      board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                    有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。本公司
      Exchange”). Its immediate parent company is Jamplan (BVI) Ltd., a               之直屬母公司為Jamplan (BVI) Ltd.，該
      limited liability company incorporated in the British Virgin Islands and        公司為一家於英屬處女群島註冊成立之
      in the opinion of the directors of the Company (the “Directors”), its           有限公司，而本公司董事（「董事」）認
      ultimate holding company is Kingboard Holdings Limited (“KHL”), an              為，本公司之最終控股公司為建滔集團
      exempted limited liability company incorporated in the Cayman Islands           有限公司（「建滔集團」，該公司為一家
      with its shares also listed on the Mainboard of the Stock Exchange.             於開曼群島註冊成立並獲豁免之有限公
      The addresses of the registered office and principal place of business          司，其股份於聯交所主板上市。本公司
      of the Company are disclosed in the section headed “Corporate                   註冊辦事處之地址及主要營業地點於本
      Information” of the annual report.                                              年報「公司資料」一節中披露。

      The Company is an investment holding company and the principal                  本公司為投資控股公司，其主要附屬公
      activities of its principal subsidiaries are set out in Note 41.                司之主要業務載於附註41。

      The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong Kong                本公司董事（「董事」）認為以港元（「港

      the users of the consolidated financial statements.                             關，故綜合財務報表以港元呈列。

2.   APPLICATION OF AMENDMENTS TO                                               2.   應用經修訂之香港財務
     HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING                                                   申報準則（「香港財務申
     STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (continued)                                                報準則」）
                                                                                         （續）

     Amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily effective                             於本年度已強制生效之經修訂之
     for the current year (continued)                                                香港財務申報準則（續）
     Impacts on application of Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKAS 8                       應用香港財務申報準則第1號及香港會
     “Definition of Material”                                                        計準則第8號（修訂本）
                                                                                               「 有關重大的定
                                                                                     義」之影響
     The Group has applied the Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKAS                         本集團於本年度首次應用香港財務申報
     8 for the first time in the current year. The amendments provide                準則第1號和香港財務申報準則第8號
     a new definition of material that states “information is material if            （修訂本）。修訂本為重要性落下新定
     omitting, misstating or obscuring it could reasonably be expected to            義，訂明「如果遺漏、錯誤陳述或掩飾
     influence decisions that the primary users of general purpose financial         某項資料可能合理預期會影響通用財務
     statements make on the basis of those financial statements, which               報表主要使用者根據該等財務報表（提
     provide financial information about a specific reporting entity.” The           供有關特定申報實體的財務資料）作出
     amendments also clarify that materiality depends on the nature or               的決策，則有關資料屬重大。」修訂本
     magnitude of information, either individually or in combination with            亦澄清重大程度取決於資料的性質或以
     other information, in the context of the financial statements taken as a        整體財務報表來看的重要程度（不論為
     whole.                                                                          個別或與其他資料結合考慮）。

     The application of the amendments in the current year had no impact             於本年度應用修訂本對綜合財務報表並
     on the consolidated financial statements.                                       無影響。

     Impacts on application of Amendments to HKFRS 3 “Definition                      應用香港財務申報準則第3號（修訂本）
     of a Business”                                                                  「有關業務的定義」之影響
     The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the                   本集團於本年度首次應用。該等修訂釐
     current year. The amendments clarify that while businesses usually               清，儘管業務通常具有產出，但產出毋
     have outputs, outputs are not required for an integrated set of                  須為一組完整的活動及資產才成為一項
     activities and assets to qualify as a business. To be considered a               業務。為符合業務定義，所收購的一組
     business, an acquired set of activities and assets must include,                 活動及資產必須至少包括投入及實質流
     at a minimum, an input and a substantive process that together                   程，且兩者結合能顯著促進創造產出的
     significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs.                       能力。

     HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING                                                       申報準則（「香港財務申
     STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (continued)                                                    報準則」）
                                                                                             （續）

     Amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily effective                                 於本年度已強制生效之經修訂之
     for the current year (continued)                                                    香港財務申報準則（續）
     Impacts on application of Amendments to HKFRS 3 “Definition                          應用香港財務申報準則第3號（修訂本）
     of a Business” (continued)                                                          「有關業務的定義」之影響（續）
     The amendments remove the assessment of whether market                               該等修訂移除對市場參與者能否取代失
     participants are capable of replacing any missing inputs or processes                去的投入或流程及繼續提供產出的評
     and continuing to produce outputs. The amendments also introduce                     估。該等修訂亦引入額外指引，有助釐
     additional guidance that helps to determine whether a substantive                    定是否已收購實質流程。
     process has been acquired.

     In addition, the amendments introduce an optional concentration                     此外，該等修訂引入一項選擇性之集中
     test that permits a simplified assessment of whether an acquired                    度測試，允許將所收購之一組活動及資
     set of activities and assets is not a business. Under the optional                  產是否屬於業務作出簡化評估。根據該
     concentration test, the acquired set of activities and assets is not                選擇性之集中度測試，若所收購的總資
     a business if substantially all of the fair value of the gross assets               產之絕大部分公平價值集中於單個可識
     acquired is concentrated in a single identifiable asset or group of                 別資產或一組類似資產，則所收購之一
     similar assets. The gross assets under assessment exclude cash and                  組活動及資產並非業務。評估下之總資
     cash equivalents, deferred tax assets, and goodwill resulting from the              產不包括現金及現金等價物、遞延稅項
     effects of deferred tax liabilities. The election on whether to apply the           資產及因遞延稅項負債的影響而產生之
     optional concentration test is available on transaction-by-transaction              商譽。選用該選擇性之集中度測試與否
     basis.                                                                              可依個別交易為基準。

     The amendments had no impact on the consolidated financial                          該等修訂對本集團綜合財務報表並無影
     statements of the Group but may impact future periods should the                    響，惟倘本集團於往後期間作出任何收
     Group make any acquisition.                                                         購則可能有所影響。

           Basis of consolidation                                                            綜合賬目基準
           The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                   綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本
           statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                          公司及其附屬公司控制之實體之
           Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the                        財務報表。當本公司符合以下情
           Company:                                                                          況，即取得控制權：

           •       has power over the investee;                                              •    有權控制被投資方；

           •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                   •    因其參與被投資方業務
                   involvement with the investee; and                                             而對可變回報承擔風險
                                                                                                  或享有權利；及

           •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                   •    有能力以其權力影響其
                                                                                                  回報。

           The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee                       倘有事實及情況顯示上列三項控
           if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to                     制權條件之其中一項或多項有
           one or more of the three elements of control listed above.                        變，本集團會重新評估其是否控
                                                                                             制被投資方。

           Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains                       當本集團取得附屬公司之控制
           control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses                       權，便將該附屬公司綜合入賬；
           control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses                      當本集團失去附屬公司之控制
           of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are                       權，便停止將該附屬公司綜合入
           included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss from                     賬。具體而言，年內收購或出售
           the date the Group gains control until the date when the                          之附屬公司之收入及支出會於本
           Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                                           集團取得控制權當日起計入綜合
                                                                                             損益表，直至本集團對該附屬公
                                                                                             司之控制權終止當日為止。

           Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income                        損益及其他全面收益的各個項目
           are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                            乃歸屬於本公司持有人及非控股
           non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of                          權益。附屬公司的全面收益總額
           subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company and                       乃歸屬於本公司持有人及非控股
           to the non-controlling interests even if this results in the non-                 權益，即使此舉會導致非控股權
           controlling interests having a deficit balance.                                   益產生虧絀結餘。

           Existence of significant financing component                                     存在重大融資部分
           In determining the transaction price, the Group adjusts the                      釐定交易價時，倘協定之付款時
           promised amount of consideration for the effects of the                          間（明示或暗示）為客戶或本集團
           time value of money if the timing of payments agreed (either                     提供有關向客戶轉讓貨品或服務
           explicitly or implicitly) provides the customer or the Group                     之重大融資利益，本集團會就貨
           with a significant benefit of financing the transfer of goods or                 幣時間價值之影響調整承諾代價
           services to the customer. In those circumstances, the contract                   金額。於該等情況下，合約即包
           contains a significant financing component. A significant                        含重大融資部分。不論融資承諾
           financing component may exist regardless of whether the                          是否明確列於合約或隱含在合約
           promise of financing is explicitly stated in the contract or                     訂約方協定之付款條款中，均可
           implied by the payment terms agreed to by the parties to the                     能存在重大融資部分。
           contract.

           For contracts where the period between payment and transfer                      對於付款與轉讓相關貨品、物業

           one year, the Group applies the practical expedient of not                       約，本集團應用可行權宜之計，
           adjusting the transaction price for any significant finance                      而不就任何重大融資部分調整交
           component.                                                                       易價。

           reporting period, with the effect of any changes in estimate                    告期間結束當日予以檢討，估計

           Inventories                                                                     存貨

           completion and costs necessary to make the sale.                                成本以及銷售所需之成本。

           At the end of the reporting period, the Group reviews the                       於報告期間結束當日，本集團須

           relevant asset is estimated in order to determine the extent of                 則估計資產可收回金額，以釐定

            Leases                                                                       租賃（續）
            Definition of a lease                                                        租賃的定義
            A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the              倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段
            right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of              時間內控制使用可識別資產的權
            time in exchange for consideration.                                          利，則該合約屬租賃或包含租
                                                                                         賃。

            For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date                  就於首次應用日期或之後訂立或
            of initial application or arising from business combinations,                修改或源於業務合併的合約而
            the Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a                       言，本集團根據香港財務申報準
            lease based on the definition under HKFRS 16 at inception,                   則第16號的定義於初始或修改
            modification date or acquisition date, as appropriate.                       或收購日期（視乎情況而定）評估
            Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and                    該合約是否為租賃或包含租賃。
            conditions of the contract are subsequently changed.                         除非合約的條款及條件其後出現
                                                                                         變動，否則有關合約將不予重新
                                                                                         評估。

            The Group as a lessee                                                        本集團作為承租人
            Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                      將代價分配至合約組成部分
            For a contract that contains a lease component and one or                    就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或
            more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group                     多項額外租賃或非租賃組成部分
            allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease                    的合約而言，本集團根據租賃組
            component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of                  成部分的相對獨立價格及非租賃
            the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price of                   組成部分的合計獨立價格基準將
            the non-lease components, including contract for acquisition                 合約代價分配至各項租賃組成部
            of ownership interests of a property which includes both                     分，包括收購含有租賃土地及非
            leasehold land and non-lease building components, unless                     租賃樓宇組成部分的物業的所有
            such allocation cannot be made reliably.                                     權權益的合約，惟有關分配無法
                                                                                         可靠作出則除外。

            The Group applies practical expedient not to separate non-                   本集團應用可行權宜之計不將非
            lease components from lease component, and instead                           租賃組成部分與租賃組成部分分
            account for the lease component and any associated non-                      開，而將租賃組成部分及任何相
            lease components as a single lease component.                                關非租賃組成部分作為單一租賃
                                                                                         組成部分入賬。

            •      the consideration for the leases increases by an                     •   租賃代價增加，增加的
                   amount commensurate with the stand-alone price                           金額相當於範圍擴大對
                   for the increase in scope and any appropriate                            應的單獨價格，加上為
                   adjustments to that stand-alone price to reflect the                     反映特定合約的實際情
                   circumstances of the particular contract.                                況而對單獨價格進行的
                                                                                            任何適當調整。

            For a lease modification that is not accounted for as a                     就未入賬為一項單獨租賃的租賃
            separate lease, the Group remeasures the lease liability based              修改而言，本集團透過使用修訂
            on the lease term of the modified lease by discounting the                  生效日期的經修訂貼現率貼現經
            revised lease payments using a revised discount rate at the                 修訂租賃付款的經修改租賃的租
            effective date of the modification.                                         期，重新計量租賃負債。

            The Group accounts for the remeasurement of lease liabilities               本集團通過對使用權資產作出相
            by making corresponding adjustments to the relevant right-                  應調整，將租賃負債的重新計量
            of-use asset. When the modified contract contains a lease                   入賬。倘經修訂合約包含租賃部
            component and one or more additional lease or non-lease                     分以及一個或多個額外租賃或非
            components, the Group allocates the consideration in the                    租賃部分，本集團按租賃部分的
            modified contract to each lease component on the basis of                   相對獨立價格以及非租賃部分的
            the relative stand-alone price of the lease component and the               獨立價格合計，將經修訂合約的
            aggregate stand-alone price of the non-lease components.                    代價分配至各租賃部分。

            Taxation                                                                      稅項
            Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                    所得稅開支指現時應繳稅項及遞
            payable and deferred tax.                                                     延稅項之總和。

            The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the                  現時應繳稅項乃按本年度應課稅
            year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax because of                溢利計算。基於其他年度應課稅
            income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other                     或可扣稅的收入或開支項目，以
            years and items that are never taxable or deductible. The                     及毋須課稅或扣稅項目，因此應
            Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates               課稅溢利與除稅前溢利不同。本
            that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end                    集團即期稅項負債乃按報告期間
            of the reporting period.                                                      結束當日已頒佈或實際已頒佈之
                                                                                          稅率計算。

            Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                           遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表資
            between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the                 產及負債賬面值與計算應課稅溢
            consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax                   利所採用相應稅基間之臨時差額
            bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax                 確認。遞延稅項負債通常會就所
            liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary                有應課稅暫時差額確認。遞延稅
            differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised                     項負債一般會就所有應課稅臨時
            for all deductible temporary difference to the extent that it is              差額確認，而遞延稅項資產一般
            probable that taxable profits will be available against which                 在可能出現可利用臨時差額扣稅
            deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                        之應課稅溢利時就所有可扣減臨
            deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the                 時差額確認。因業務合併以外交
            temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other               易初步確認資產及負債且不影響
            than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in                  應課稅溢利亦不影響會計溢利而
            a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the                 引致之臨時差額，則不會確認該
            accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not              等遞延稅項資產及負債。此外，
            recognised if the temporary difference arises from the initial                不會就因初步確認商譽而引致之
            recognition of goodwill.                                                      臨時差額確認遞延稅項負債。

            Taxation (continued)                                                                  稅項（續）
            Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary                         遞延稅項負債乃按與於附屬公司
            differences associated with investments in subsidiaries,                              之投資有關之應課稅臨時差額而
            except where the Group is able to control the reversal of the                         確認，惟若本集團可控制臨時差
            temporary difference and it is probable that the temporary                            額之撥回及臨時差額有可能不會
            difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred                       於可見將來撥回之情況則除外。
            tax assets arising from deductible temporary differences                              就確認該等投資之相關可扣減暫
            associated with such investments are only recognised to                               時差額所產生之遞延稅項資產而
            the extent that it is probable that there will be sufficient                          言，其以很可能取得足夠之應課

            temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in the                         運用，且有關暫時差額預期於可
            foreseeable future.                                                                   見將來撥回之情況為限。

            The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the                         遞延稅項資產之賬面值於各報告
            end of each reporting period and reduced to the extent that                           期間結束當日作檢討，並在沒可
            it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be                      能會有足夠應課稅溢利收回全部
            available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.                          或部分資產時加以遞減。

            Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax                           根據報告期間結束當日已頒佈或
            rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the                           實際已頒佈之稅率（及稅法），遞
            liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate (and                 延稅項資產及負債按負債清償或
            tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively enacted by                          資產變現期間預期適用之稅率計
            the end of the reporting period.                                                      量。

            The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects                       遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反映
            the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                             本集團預期於報告期間結束當日
            which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period,                          將出現的稅務後果，以收回資產
            to recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and                            或清償負債之賬面值。
            liabilities.

            The effective interest method is a method of calculating                       實際利率法是一種計算金融資產
            the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability                 或金融負債攤銷成本與相關期間
            and of allocating interest income and interest expense over                    分派利息收入及利息支出之方
            the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate                   法。實際利率是於金融資產或金
            that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                      融負債的估計可使用年期或（如
            payments (including all fees and points paid or received that                  適用）更短期間將估計未來現金
            form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction              收入及付款（包括所有已付或已
            costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected                    收並構成實際利率一部分之費
            life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where                  用、交易成本及其他溢價或折
            appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on                   價）精確貼現至初步確認時之賬
            initial recognition.                                                           面淨值之比率。

            Interest income from debt instruments at fair value through                    於本集團日常業務過程中來自按
            other comprehensive income (“FVTOCI”) and dividend income                      公平值計入其他全面收益的債務
            from equity instruments which are derived from the Group’s                     工具的利息收入及來自權益工具
            ordinary course of business are presented as revenue. Interest                 的股息收入呈列為營業額。銀行
            income from bank balances and deposits are included in                         結餘及存款利息收入計入其他收
            other income, gains and losses.                                                入、收益及虧損。

                   Financial assets at FVTPL are measured at fair value                           按公平值計入損益的金

                   For a financial guarantee contract, the Group is                               對於財務擔保合約，僅

                   consideration past due information and relevant credit                          合考慮逾期信息和相關
                   information such as forward looking macroeconomic                               信貸信息（如前瞻性宏觀
                   information.                                                                    經濟信息）的基礎上考慮
                                                                                                   的。

                   For collective assessment, the Group takes into                                 就綜合評估而言，本集
                   consideration the following characteristics when                                團劃分組別時考慮以下
                   formulating the grouping:                                                       特徵：

                   •       Past-due status;                                                        •    逾期狀況；

                   •       Nature, size and industry of debtors; and                               •    債務人的性質、

      Operating Decision Maker (“CODM”) in order to allocate resources to                   分部作資源分配及評估其表現上所定期
      segments and to assess their performance. Specifically, the Group’s                   審閱的有關集團不同部門之內部報告作
      reportable segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into three                            為確定經營分部之基準。具體而言，於
      main operating divisions – (i) manufacturing and sale of laminates;                   香港財務申報準則第8號下，本集團之
      (ii) properties; and (iii) investments. The management aggregated                     須予呈報之分部分為三個主要經營分
      the sales of properties, rental and hotel income business into one                    部－(i)製造及銷售覆銅面板(ii)物業及(iii)
      reportable segment because the financial performance of these                         投資。管理層將物業銷售，物業出租及
      businesses are affected by changes in the property market.                            酒店收入業務合併為一個可報告分部，
                                                                                            因為這些業務的財務表現受到物業市場
                                                                                            變動的影響。

14. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES                                                       14. 投資物業
                                                                                                HK$’000
                                                                                                 千港元


       FAIR VALUE                                                    公平值
       At 1 January 2019                                             於二零一九年一月一日                1,387,842
       Exchange adjustments                                          匯兌調整                          (3,046)
       Additions                                                     添置                           11,899
       Gain on fair value changes                                    公平值變動收益                        5,600


       At 31 December 2019                                           於二零一九年十二月三十一日             1,402,295
       Exchange adjustments                                          匯兌調整                         36,510
       Loss on fair value changes                                    公平值變動虧損                        (742)


       At 31 December 2020                                           於二零二零年十二月三十一日             1,438,063


      The Group leases out various offices under operating leases with            本集團根據經營租賃出租多項辦公室，
      rentals payable monthly. The leases typically run for a fixed period of     每月收取租金。租賃一般附有介乎1至
      1 to 17 years (2019: 1 to 17 years).                                        17年的固定租期（二零一九年：1至17
                                                                                  年）。

      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of the        本集團不會因租賃安排而承擔外匯風
      lease arrangement, as all leases are denominated in the respective          險，因為所有租賃均以集團實體各自的
      functional currencies of group entities. The lease contracts do not         功能貨幣列值。租約並不包括餘值擔保
      contain residual value guarantee and/or lessee’s option to purchase         及╱或承租人於租期末購買物業的選擇
      the property at the end of lease term.                                      權。

      The fair value of the Group’s investment properties at 31 December          本集團投資物業於二零二零年十二月
      2020 had been arrived at on the basis of a valuation carried out by         三十一日之公平值乃按與本集團並無關
      Ravia Global Appraisal Advisory Limited (2019: Ravia Global Appraisal       連之獨立合資格估值師瑞豐環球評估
      Advisory Limited), an independent qualified valuer not connected to         諮詢有限公司（二零一九年：瑞豐環球
      the Group. Ravia Global Appraisal Advisory Limited is a member of           評估諮詢有限公司）進行估值之基準達
      the Hong Kong Institute of Surveyors. The valuation was determined          致。瑞豐環球評估諮詢有限公司為香港
      based on direct comparison method by reference to the market                測量師學會會員。估值乃按直接比較法
      observable transactions of similar properties and adjusted to reflect       參考類似物業之可觀察市場交易並作出
      conditions and locations of subject properties. There has been no           調整以反映目標物業之狀況及位置後釐
      change from the valuation technique used in prior years.                    定。於過往年度所用之估值技術並無變
                                                                                  動。

      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the               為釐定相關物業的公平值，集團管理層
      management of the Group determines the appropriate valuation                就公平值計量釐定適當的估值技術及輸
      techniques and inputs for fair value measurements.                          入數據。

      to the model. The persons in charge of financial matters report the         宜負責人每季向董事匯報管理層之發

      subsidiary of Kingboard Copper Foil Holdings Limited (“KBCF” or the         月三十日及二零一五年八月二十八日，

                                                                                       （建滔銅箔為本集團一間附屬

32. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                     32. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will      本集團管理其資本以確保通過優化債務
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to        與股本結存為權益持有人爭取最高回
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.        報，使本集團旗下實體能夠持續經營。
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.              本集團之整體策略與過往年度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings               誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團之
      and lease liabilities, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity           資本結構包括銀行借貸及租賃負債（扣
      attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share               除現金及現金等價物）及本公司持有人
      capital, other reserves and retained profits as disclosed in the             應佔權益（包括已發行股本、其他儲備
      consolidated statement of changes in equity.                                 及保留溢利）。

      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.           董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and       部分，董事將考慮資本成本，及與各類
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the       別資本相關之風險。董事認為，本集團
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through      將通過支付股息、發行新股、購回股
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as            份、發行新債或者贖回現有債項，以平
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.            衡整體資本結構。

            to monitor the price risk and will consider hedging the risk                      於若干無報價股本證券，為從事

            considered as insignificant.

      The Group participates in both a defined contribution scheme                    本集團同時參加一項根據職業退休計劃
      which was registered under the Occupational Retirement Scheme                   條例註冊之定額供款計劃（「職業退休計
